### Video Information

### Description
{% raw %}
From a now deleted stream. This is a long video, I've cut as much dead air as possible. If you don't want to finish it, watch the last 3 minutes.


I've been holding off posting anything since it had been asked not to publicise anything relating to Marty until after his funeral which was earlier today.

Dooley however, streamed for 15hrs a day for 3 days after he found out. He was told he had died, by samy, and instead of not saying anything he decided to pretend he was predicting his death, and if it came true it would be evidence of his divinity.

He also stream sniped the funeral after being encouraged to do so by his mods. He made crass jokes throughout and claimed victory over a man who had died. 

Marty once said that he disliked Tim Dooley even more than Cyraxx.

It's hard to argue with him.
{% endraw %}
